# awesome-iclr-2026-artist

[![Awesome](https://cdn.rawgit.com/sindresorhus/awesome/d7305f38d29fed78fa85652e3a63e154dd8e8829/media/badge.svg)](https://github.com/Mondrian-He/Awesome-artist)
[![GitHub Repo stars](https://img.shields.io/github/stars/Mondrian-He/awesome-iclr-2026-artist)](https://github.com/Mondrian-He/awesome-iclr-2026-artist)
![ICLR 2026](https://img.shields.io/badge/ICLR%202026-green) ![Artist](https://img.shields.io/badge/Artist-purple) ![Main Conference](https://img.shields.io/badge/Main%20Conference-orange) ![Visuals](https://img.shields.io/badge/Workflow%2C%20Framework%2C%20Table%2C%20etc.-pink)

> [!IMPORTANT]
>
> ***For other conferences such as NeurIPS, ICLR, ICML, EMNLP, and ACL, check out [Awesome-artist](https://github.com/Mondrian-He/Awesome-artist)! 🤩***

> [!NOTE]
>
> This repository collects framework diagrams, experimental figures, and tables from ICLR 2026 main conference papers to study their visual presentation techniques. The accepted-paper list contains **5351 papers**, organized as **Oral → Poster (Regular)**. Visual extraction is complete for **5347 papers**; **4 papers remain pending**. To keep the visual collection browsable, it is split into **199 Markdown parts**, with approximately 27 papers per part. Hope we can make progress together! 😁

---

> [!IMPORTANT]
>
> **Papers 1 to 224 are Oral.**  
> **Papers 225 to 5351 are Poster (Regular).**  
> **No papers are labeled Spotlight in the current OpenReview metadata.**
>
> Paper numbers refer to the index below.

## 📚 Complete Paper Index

- **Total accepted papers:** 5351
- **Papers with completed extraction:** 5347
- **Pending extraction:** 4
- **Total images:** 68719
- **Total tables:** 56968
- **Total visual assets:** 125687

Split into **199 parts** for better browsing. Each paper title below links directly to its section.
